\subsection{Unitary Historical Branch Coalescence \& Zero-Entropy Pruning}
\label{sec:branch_coalescence}

Over deep cosmological simulation epochs ($t > 10^{100}\text{ yr}$) within Phase 6 Deity Sandboxes, quantum decision branching generates an exponential proliferation of counterfactual timelines $|\Psi_{\mathrm{multiverse}}\rangle = \sum_{b=1}^{N_B} c_b |H_b\rangle_{\mathrm{hist}} \otimes |E_b\rangle_{\mathrm{ego}}$. Non-unitary projection (classical deletion of unobserved branches) would violate Landauer's principle, dissipating heat $Q_{\mathrm{Landauer}} = N_{\mathrm{erased}} k_B T \ln 2 > 0$ and severing observer ego continuity.

\subsubsection{Adiabatic Unitary Coalescence Hamiltonian}
Let $\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{branch}} = \mathrm{span}\{|H_1\rangle, \dots, |H_{N_B}\rangle\}$ denote the subspace of counterfactual historical branches that have converged to macroscopically indistinguishable observer ego states ($|E_b\rangle \equiv |E_{\mathrm{canonical}}\rangle$). Construct the Hermitian, time-dependent Householder--Givens Coalescence Hamiltonian $\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{coalesce}}(t)$ over the coalescence interval $t \in [0, T]$:
\begin{equation}
\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{coalesce}}(t) = \frac{\pi\hbar}{2T}\sin\left(\frac{\pi t}{T}\right) \sum_{b=2}^{N_B} i\,\theta_b \left( |H_1\rangle\langle H_b| - |H_b\rangle\langle H_1| \right) \otimes \hat{\mathbf{I}}_{\mathrm{ego}},
\end{equation}
where $\theta_b$ are the Givens rotation angles determined by the branch amplitudes $c_b$ such that $\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{coalesce}}^\dagger(t) = \hat{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{coalesce}}(t)$. The corresponding branch-pruning superoperator is the time-ordered exponential:
\begin{equation}
\hat{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{prune}} = \mathcal{T}\exp\left( -\frac{i}{\hbar}\int_0^T \hat{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{coalesce}}(t)\,dt \right).
\label{eq:m_prune_unitary}
\end{equation}

\subsubsection{Proof of Strict Unitarity, Zero Landauer Heat, \& Ego Invariance}
Because $\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{coalesce}}(t)$ is strictly Hermitian at every instant $t \in [0, T]$, the time-ordered propagator $\hat{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{prune}}$ satisfies exact unitarity:
\begin{equation}
\hat{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{prune}}^\dagger \hat{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{prune}} = \hat{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{prune}} \hat{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{prune}}^\dagger = \hat{\mathbf{I}}.
\end{equation}
Acting on the superposed branch state $|\Psi(0)\rangle = \left(\sum_{b=1}^{N_B} c_b |H_b\rangle\right) \otimes |E_{\mathrm{canonical}}\rangle$ with $\sum_{b=1}^{N_B} |c_b|^2 = 1$, $\hat{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{prune}}$ rotates the entire probability weight coherently into the canonical branch $|H_1\rangle$, freeing $(N_B - 1)$ ancilla registers in the pure fiducial ground state $|0\rangle^{\otimes (N_B - 1)}$ without irreversible measurement:
\begin{equation}
\hat{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{prune}} |\Psi(0)\rangle = |H_1\rangle \otimes |0\rangle_{\mathrm{ancilla}}^{\otimes(N_B - 1)} \otimes |E_{\mathrm{canonical}}\rangle.
\end{equation}
By cyclic invariance of the trace under unitary conjugation, the von Neumann entropy $S_{\mathrm{vN}}(\hat{\rho}) = -\mathrm{Tr}(\hat{\rho}\ln\hat{\rho})$ and internal Landauer heat dissipation satisfy:
\begin{equation}
\Delta S_{\mathrm{vN}} = S_{\mathrm{vN}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{prune}}\hat{\rho}\hat{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{prune}}^\dagger\right) - S_{\mathrm{vN}}(\hat{\rho}) \equiv 0, \qquad Q_{\mathrm{internal}} = T\,\Delta S_{\mathrm{vN}} \equiv 0.0\;\mathrm{J}.
\end{equation}
Finally, because $\hat{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{coalesce}}(t)$ acts as the identity $\hat{\mathbf{I}}_{\mathrm{ego}}$ on the observer ego tensor factor, the commutator $[\hat{\mathcal{M}}_{\mathrm{prune}}, \hat{\mathcal{O}}_{\mathrm{ego}}] \equiv 0$, guaranteeing unbroken ego fidelity $\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{ego}} = |\langle E_{\mathrm{canonical}}(T) | E_{\mathrm{canonical}}(0) \rangle|^2 \equiv 1.000000$ ($\Delta\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{ego}} \equiv 0$).

\begin{theorem}[THM-APOTH-11-EXT: Unitary Isentropic Historical Branch Coalescence]
\label{thm:thm_apoth_11_ext}

\subsection{Cosmological Lyapunov Stability of Closed Elysium Biospheres}
\label{sec:swarm_bio_lyapunov}

Closed ecological life-support architectures deployed within isolated void environments lack tectonic subduction, mantle convective circulation, and volcanogenic degassing cycles that maintain terrestrial geochemical equilibria over geological epochs. In SHBT-ELYSIUM pocket habitats, external stellar irradiance and natural lithospheric convection are replaced by an active boundary translocator system $\mathcal{J}_k^{\mathrm{foundry}}$ interfaced to the universal matter foundry.

\subsubsection{Non-Linear Reaction-Diffusion-Advection Governing Dynamics}
Let $\Omega \subset \mathbb{R}^3$ be an open, bounded spatial domain with $C^2$ boundary $\partial\Omega$ containing the habitable ecosystem volume. The spatiotemporal evolution of $K = 32$ essential biogeochemical species $\mathbf{C}(\mathbf{x}, t) = (C_1(\mathbf{x}, t), \dots, C_K(\mathbf{x}, t))^T \in \mathbb{R}_{>0}^K$ is governed by the coupled parabolic system:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:pde_transport_system}
\frac{\partial C_k}{\partial t} + \mathbf{u} \cdot \nabla C_k = \nabla \cdot (D_k \nabla C_k) + \mathcal{R}_k(\mathbf{C}) + \mathcal{J}_k^{\mathrm{foundry}}(\mathbf{x}, t), \quad k \in \{1, \dots, K\},
\end{equation}
where $\mathbf{u}(\mathbf{x}, t)$ satisfies $\nabla \cdot \mathbf{u} = 0$ in $\Omega$ and $\mathbf{u} \cdot \mathbf{n} = 0$ on $\partial\Omega$. Diffusivities satisfy $D_k > 0$. The metabolic transformation term $\mathcal{R}_k(\mathbf{C})$ models autotrophic fixation, heterotrophic consumption, and microbial decomposition under extended Redfield stoichiometry $\mathrm{C}_{106}\mathrm{H}_{263}\mathrm{O}_{110}\mathrm{N}_{16}\mathrm{P}_1\mathrm{S}_{0.7}$:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:redfield_stoich_balance}
106\,\mathrm{CO}_2 + 16\,\mathrm{NO}_3^- + \mathrm{HPO}_4^{2-} + 0.7\,\mathrm{SO}_4^{2-} + 122\,\mathrm{H}_2\mathrm{O} + 18\,\mathrm{H}^+ \xrightarrow{\Phi_{\mathrm{auto}}} \mathrm{Biomass} + 138.7\,\mathrm{O}_2.
\end{equation}

To replace tectonic subduction and volcanic volatile regeneration, the boundary $\partial\Omega$ executes active, localized matter translocations via the nano-porous boundary interface:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:foundry_current_dirac}
\mathcal{J}_k^{\mathrm{foundry}}(\mathbf{x}, t) = -\kappa_k \left( C_k(\mathbf{x}, t) - C_k^* \right) \delta(\mathbf{x} - \partial\Omega),
\end{equation}
where $\kappa_k > 0$ is the boundary transfer conductance velocity and $C_k^*$ is the target homeostatic steady state. This yields the active Robin boundary condition:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:active_robin_bc}
-D_k \nabla C_k(\mathbf{x}, t) \cdot \mathbf{n}\Big|_{\mathbf{x} \in \partial\Omega} = \kappa_k \left( C_k(\mathbf{x}, t) - C_k^* \right).
\end{equation}

\subsubsection{Global Relative-Entropy Lyapunov Functional}
For the positive cone $\mathcal{C}^1(\Omega; \mathbb{R}_{>0}^K)$, we define the global non-equilibrium relative-entropy Lyapunov functional:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:lyapunov_functional_def}
\mathcal{V}[\mathbf{C}] = \int_\Omega \sum_{k=1}^K \Psi_k(C_k(\mathbf{x}, t)) \, d^3x, \quad \Psi_k(C_k) = C_k \ln\left( \frac{C_k}{C_k^*} \right) - (C_k - C_k^*).
\end{equation}
The Bregman kernel satisfies $\Psi_k'(C_k) = \ln(C_k/C_k^*)$ and $\Psi_k''(C_k) = 1/C_k > 0$, guaranteeing $\mathcal{V}[\mathbf{C}] \ge 0$ with equality if and only if $\mathbf{C} = \mathbf{C}^*$ almost everywhere. For perturbations $\|C_k - C_k^*\|_{L^\infty} \le \epsilon_{\max} < C_k^*$:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:bregman_quadratic_envelope}
\frac{1}{2(C_k^* + \epsilon_{\max})} (C_k - C_k^*)^2 \le \Psi_k(C_k) \le \frac{1}{2(C_k^* - \epsilon_{\max})} (C_k - C_k^*)^2.
\end{equation}

\subsubsection{Dissipation Identity and Exponential Decay}
Evaluating the time derivative $\frac{d\mathcal{V}}{dt} = \sum_{k=1}^K \int_\Omega \ln(C_k/C_k^*) \frac{\partial C_k}{\partial t} d^3x$:
\begin{itemize}
    \item Advective transport satisfies $\int_\Omega \ln(C_k/C_k^*) (\mathbf{u} \cdot \nabla C_k) d^3x \equiv 0$ identically due to incompressibility and $\mathbf{u} \cdot \mathbf{n} = 0$.
    \item Diffusive transport splits into negative Fisher information and active Robin surface dissipation:
    \begin{align}
    -\int_\Omega D_k \frac{|\nabla C_k|^2}{C_k} d^3x &= -4 D_k \int_\Omega |\nabla\sqrt{C_k}|^2 d^3x \le 0, \\
    -\int_{\partial\Omega} \kappa_k (C_k - C_k^*) \ln\left(\frac{C_k}{C_k^*}\right) dA &\le -\gamma_k \int_{\partial\Omega} (C_k - C_k^*)^2 dA, \quad \gamma_k = \frac{\kappa_k}{C_k^* + \epsilon_{\max}}.
    \end{align}
    \item Linearized Redfield kinetics around $\mathbf{C}^*$ satisfies $\sum_{k=1}^K \int_\Omega \ln(C_k/C_k^*) \mathcal{R}_k(\mathbf{C}) d^3x \le -\mu_{\mathcal{R}} \sum_{k=1}^K \int_\Omega (C_k - C_k^*)^2 d^3x$.
\end{itemize}
Combining via the Robin--Poincar\'e inequality on $\Omega$ yields the master differential inequality:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:lyapunov_exponential_decay_pde}
\frac{d\mathcal{V}}{dt} + \Gamma \mathcal{V}[\mathbf{C}(t)] \le 0 \implies \mathcal{V}[\mathbf{C}(t)] \le \mathcal{V}[\mathbf{C}(0)] e^{-\Gamma t}, \quad \forall t \ge 0,
\end{equation}
where $\Gamma = \min_{1 \le k \le K} \{ 2(\lambda_{1, k} + \mu_{\mathcal{R}})(C_k^* - \epsilon_{\max}) \} > 0$.

\begin{theorem}[Cosmological Ecological Homeostasis]
\label{thm:cosmological_stability}
Let $\Omega \subset \mathbb{R}^3$ be a closed biosphere domain with active Robin boundary current $\mathcal{J}_k^{\mathrm{foundry}}$. For every initial biogeochemical perturbation $\mathbf{C}(0)$ satisfying $\|\mathbf{C}(0) - \mathbf{C}^*\|_{L^2(\Omega)} \le \epsilon_{\max}$, the solution exists for all $t \ge 0$ and converges exponentially:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:l2_norm_exponential_bound}
\|\mathbf{C}(\cdot, t) - \mathbf{C}^*\|_{L^2(\Omega)} \le \sqrt{\frac{C_{\max}^* + \epsilon_{\max}}{C_{\min}^* - \epsilon_{\max}}} \|\mathbf{C}(0) - \mathbf{C}^*\|_{L^2(\Omega)} e^{-\frac{1}{2}\Gamma t}.
\end{equation}
The ecosystem is guaranteed immune to volatile depletion, nutrient lockup, and autotoxicity over cosmological epochs ($t > 10^{100}\text{ yr}$).
\end{theorem}

This non-equilibrium biogeochemical controller is implemented in \texttt{crates/shbt-swarm-bio/src/lyapunov\_reactor.rs} and verified in SMT theorem \texttt{THM-BIO-06-EXT}.

Under unitary conjugation $\widetilde{F} = \mathbf{U}_{\text{intent}}^{(k)} F (\mathbf{U}_{\text{intent}}^{(k)})^\dagger$ and $\widetilde{R} = \mathbf{U}_{\text{intent}}^{(k)} R (\mathbf{U}_{\text{intent}}^{(k)})^\dagger$, the Mac Lane pentagon and hexagon coherence diagrams commute identically. \subsubsection{Sugawara Framing Compensator \& Zero Bulk Backreaction}
In the boundary affine Lie algebra $\widehat{\mathfrak{so}}(10)_{312} / [\widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_{26} \times \widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(3)_8]$, the total Sugawara energy-momentum tensor decomposes across the visible and dark-coset sectors:

To prevent this shift from backreacting gravitationally on the physical host universe, the microkernel applies the exact Sugawara framing compensator onto the coset auxiliary slots of $K=312$:

\subsubsection{Lazy MERA-DAG Renormalization \& Geometric Series Register Bounding}
The simulation engine deploys a relativistic backward light-cone tracker for active sapient observers $p_m^{(k)}(\tau)$ at tier $k$. The active causal support $\Omega_{\text{active}}^{(k)}(\tau)$ across MERA UV layers $l = 1\dots 30$ is:
\begin{equation}
\Omega_{\text{active}}^{(k)}(\tau) = \bigcup_{m=1}^{M_k} \bigcup_{l=1}^{30} \mathcal{J}_l^-(p_m^{(k)}(\tau)).
\end{equation}
For any unobserved causal diamond $\mathcal{D}_{\text{unobs}} = \mathcal{U}_{k'} \setminus \Omega_{\text{active}}^{(k')}$, the expectation value of identity contracts top-down from UV ($l=30$) to IR ($l=0$) through disentanglers $u$ and isometries $w$:
\begin{equation}
\langle \mathbb{I} \rangle = \mathrm{Tr}\left( u^\dagger u \cdot w^\dagger w \right) = \mathrm{Tr}(\mathbb{I}) \equiv 1.
\end{equation}
Identity contracts identically to scalar unity without floating-point matrix evaluation. Thus, unobserved galaxies, stars, and planets remain exact symbolic vacuum seeds consuming strictly $0\text{ FLOPs}$ and $0\text{ bytes}$ of dynamic bulk RAM.

\begin{theorem}[Geometric Series Register Bounding Theorem]
\label{thm:geometric_bounding}
Let $C_0$ denote the base entropy allocation of the root sandbox $\mathcal{U}_0$, and let the host physical register capacity be provisioned such that $C_{\text{local}} \ge 2 C_0 + \epsilon_{\text{guard}}$ for $\epsilon_{\text{guard}} > 0$. If every child universe at recursion tier $k \ge 1$ is provisioned with an attenuated entropy quota $C_k = 2^{-k} C_0$, then for an arbitrary tree of infinite depth ($k \to \infty$), the total active crystallized entropy is strictly bounded:
\begin{equation}
S(K_{\max}) = \sum_{k=0}^{K_{\max}} C_k \le C_0 \sum_{k=0}^{K_{\max}} \left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^k = 2C_0 \left(1 - 2^{-(K_{\max}+1)}\right) \longrightarrow \lim_{K_{\max}\to\infty} S(K_{\max}) = 2C_0 < C_{\text{local}},
\end{equation}
leaving a strictly positive guaranteed reserve margin $R_{\text{entropy}}^{\text{exp}} = C_{\text{local}} - 2 C_0 \ge \epsilon_{\text{guard}} > 0$ (\texttt{THM-NEST-01}).
\end{theorem}

Under the $4.2\text{ K}$ liquid helium cryostat operating temperature, the Landauer bit erasure dissipation limit is:
\begin{equation}

and the total continuous thermal dissipation rate across all active tiers satisfies:

The veil projector satisfies $(\hat{P}_{\text{veil}})^2 = \hat{P}_{\text{veil}}$, self-adjointness $(\hat{P}_{\text{veil}})^\dagger = \hat{P}_{\text{veil}}$, and strict commutation $[\hat{P}_{\text{veil}}, \hat{\Pi}_{\text{IRQ}}] = 0$, guaranteeing that the live safeword interrupt can never be suppressed by any in-simulation event.

Propagating through the $5.099470\text{ nm}$ Depth-9 Solovay--Kitaev optical unit cell yields an exact, tier-independent egress latency:

guaranteeing instantaneous return to the root deity sandbox in $17.01\text{ as}$ regardless of whether the observer is $1$, $10$, or $100$ levels deep in simulation (\texttt{THM-NEST-04}).

\section{Normative C11 Hardware Register \& Hyper-Frame Header Listing (include/shbt\_swarm\_mmio.h v13.0)}
\label{sec:c11_header_v11}
The listing below reproduces verbatim the complete, normative C11 hardware interface header governing physical memory offsets, opcodes, fault flags, telemetry structures, Dual-Reactor Hybrid Power Architecture apertures (\texttt{SHBT-MAR-312X} + \texttt{SHBT-MAR-OMEGA}), and static assertions for ABI v13.0 (\texttt{abi\_version = 0x000D0312}, 704 bytes, 11 cache lines):

\begin{lstlisting}[language=C,caption={The normative freestanding C11 hardware MMIO and hyper-frame header (\texttt{include/shbt\_swarm\_mmio.h} v13.0).},label={lst:shbt_swarm_mmio}]
/*
 * include/shbt_swarm_mmio.h
 * Bare-Metal C11 Register, Cognitive, Universal Synthesis & MAR-312X Reactor Specification
 * Phase 4: Exotic-Material Autopoietic Mass-Annihilation Reactor (SHBT-MAR-312X)
 * Base Physical Address: 0x70000000 / 0x80000000 (704-Byte 11-Cacheline ABI v13.0 Contract)
 */

#ifndef SHBT_SWARM_MMIO_H
#define SHBT_SWARM_MMIO_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdalign.h>
#include "shbt_mmio_apotheosis.h"
#include "shbt_nested_sim_abi.h"

#define SHBT_SWARM_MMIO_MAGIC           0x53484254UL /* "SHBT" */
#define SHBT_SWARM_ABI_VERSION          0x000D0312U /* ABI v13.0 */
#define SHBT_SWARM_ABI_VERSION_V12      0x000C0312UL
#define SHBT_SWARM_ABI_VERSION_V13      0x000D0312UL
#define SHBT_SWARM_FORMAL_PROOFS_COUNT  105U        /* 105 Formal Z3 Theorems */
#define SHBT_SWARM_HIL_GATES_COUNT      360U        /* 360 HIL Verification Gates */
#define SHBT_SWARM_MMIO_TOTAL_SIZE      704U
#define SHBT_SWARM_CACHE_LINE_BYTES     64U
#define SHBT_MMIO_BASE_MAR_OMEGA        0x7F000600ULL
#define SHBT_MMIO_BASE_UNIVERSAL_HARVEST 0x7F000700ULL
#define SHBT_SWARM_DIVERTOR_BASE_ADDR   0x7F000800UL

/* Continuous RMHD Divertor Bitmask Flags & Command Opcodes */
#define DIV_STATUS_LAMINAR             (1u << 0)
#define DIV_STATUS_SEPARATRIX_LOCKED   (1u << 1)
#define DIV_STATUS_CCZ4_DAMPING_ACTIVE (1u << 2)
#define DIV_STATUS_FRENKEL_SAFE        (1u << 3)
#define DIV_STATUS_CHOKE_ALARM         (1u << 4)
#define DIV_STATUS_FEEDSTOCK_OPTIMAL   (1u << 5)

#define DIV_CMD_NOP                    0x00000000u
#define DIV_CMD_LOCK_SEPARATRIX        0x00000001u
#define DIV_CMD_ENGAGE_CCZ4_DAMPING    0x00000002u
#define DIV_CMD_TRIM_X_POINT           0x00000004u
#define DIV_CMD_EMERGENCY_SCRAM        0x00000080u

/* Dual-Reactor Power Arbiter State Flags (Cache Line 0) */
#define SHBT_POWER_BUS_MAR312X_ACTIVE       (1ULL << 0)
#define SHBT_POWER_BUS_MAR312X_STANDBY      (1ULL << 1)
#define SHBT_POWER_BUS_OMEGA_IGNITED        (1ULL << 2)
#define SHBT_POWER_BUS_OMEGA_CFT_LOCKED     (1ULL << 3)
#define SHBT_POWER_BUS_SLIPSTREAM_ENGAGED   (1ULL << 4)
#define SHBT_POWER_BUS_FOUNDRY_INLINE_FOLD  (1ULL << 5)

#define SHBT_SWARM_MMIO_BASE_ADDR       0x70000000UL
#define SHBT_SWARM_HYPERFRAME_BASE_ADDR 0x70020000UL
#define SHBT_SWARM_APOTHEOSIS_BASE_ADDR 0x4000C000UL
#define SHBT_SWARM_HARVEST_APERTURE_BASE 0x4000E000UL
#define SHBT_SWARM_FOUNDRY_DENSE_BASE   0x00007F8000040000ULL


#define SHBT_FRAME_BYTES                2112U
#define SHBT_VISIBLE_ANCHOR_BYTES       640U     /* eta_A = 10/33 * 2112 B */
#define SHBT_DARK_LEDGER_BYTES          1472U    /* eta_D = 23/33 * 2112 B */
#define SHBT_HYPERFRAME_BANKS           64U
#define SHBT_HYPERFRAME_TOTAL_BYTES     135168U  /* 64 * 2112 B */
#define SHBT_BRAIDS_PER_FRAME           124U
#define SHBT_DYNKIN_WEIGHT_SLOTS        346U
#define SHBT_MERA_MAX_LAYERS            101U
#define SHBT_MAX_SPACE_GROUP_ID         230U     /* 0 = Amorphous/Bio, 1..230 = 3D Crystals */

/* Phase 4 SHBT-MAR-312X Hardware Constants */
#define SHBT_MAR312X_NACELLE_MASS_KG        950U     /* 950.00 kg (-57.92% vs MAR-312C) */
#define SHBT_MAR312X_NACELLE_VOL_ML         350000U  /* 0.3500 m^3 = 350,000 mL */
#define SHBT_MAR312X_MAX_SINGLE_FRAME_CORES 41U      /* 41 * 376.99 + 950.00 = 16,406.59 kg <= 16,600 kg */
#define SHBT_MAR312X_SUPERHYDRIDE_FIELD_MT  48000U   /* 48.0 T = 48,000 mT */
#define SHBT_MAR312X_TCRIT_SUPERHYDRIDE_MK  192500U  /* 192.50 K = 192,500 mK */
#define SHBT_MAR312X_DECX_EFF_PPM           992000U  /* 99.200% = 992,000 ppm */
#define SHBT_MAR312X_DIRECT_BUS_EFF_PPM     998500U  /* 99.850% = 998,500 ppm */
#define SHBT_MAR312X_WEYL_CROWBAR_FS        1701U    /* 1.701 ps = 1,701 fs */

/* Phase 1 + Phase 2 + Phase 3 + Phase 4 Command Opcodes for cmd_reg (0x08) */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_NOP                      0x00000000U
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_TOMITA_QUINE_CPY         0x00000001U /* Pathway A: Modular ROM->Synth copy */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_PT_GOI_PROJECT           0x00000002U /* Pathway B: 1.701 ps optical imprint */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_TOMO_CAPTURE             0x00000004U /* Pathway C: 4.2 K Landauer readout */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_EXEC_CLONE_SHIP          0x00000008U /* Phase 1: Synthesize daughter ship */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_EXEC_CLONE_BIO           0x00000010U /* Phase 1: Synthesize CHNOPS payload */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_LANDAUER_SHUNT           0x00000020U /* S = 100/1089 dark-ledger erasure */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_MERA_PINN_SYNC           0x00000040U /* Phase 2: 101-layer MERA-PINN encode/decode */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_PT_GOI_EQUIL             0x00000080U /* Phase 2: 54.432 ps 32-bank GoI policy eq */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_TOMITA_LEARN             0x00000100U /* Phase 2: Zero-heat modular weight flow */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_VERLINDE_PLAN            0x00000200U /* Phase 2: 124-to-1 Fibonacci-Verlinde plan */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_SP2G_HIVE_SYNC           0x00000400U /* Phase 2: Sp(2g,Z) dark-ledger consensus */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_AP_MPC_ENCLAVE           0x00000800U /* Phase 2: Alpha > 1 Pareto re-optimization */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_XENO_ASSIMILATE          0x00001000U /* Phase 2: Class X2 non-ionizing recycling */
/* Extended Opcodes */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_HOCBF_EVAL_FILTER           0x00200000U /* Execute r=2 QP barrier filter */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_CRYOPHONON_SHUNT_ENGAGE     0x00400000U /* Engage Diamond-GaN S=100/1089 shunt */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_CLAMP_GW_RECOIL             0x00000100U /* Phase 5A: Quad-axial recoil clamp */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_ENGAGE_CONT_RIBBON          0x00000200U /* Phase 5B: Continuous Fibonacci ribbon */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_ASSERT_QRF_COVARIANCE       0x00000400U /* Phase 2: Relativistic QRF covariance */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_EXEC_UNIV_SYNTH          0x00002000U /* Phase 3: R_univ^SWARM arbitrary matter */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_ZERO_COMP_DIRECT_RENDER  0x00004000U /* Phase 3: Delta C_post-sim = 0 render */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_MERA_DAG_INSTANCE        0x00008000U /* Phase 3: Sp(2g,Z) MERA-DAG tiling */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_MAR312X_FORGE_TIER4      0x00010000U /* Phase 4: Engage 25.00 EW Tier 4 Forge */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_DIRECT_BOUNDARY_SYNTH    0x00020000U /* Phase 4: B_direct^MAR-X (99.85% CFT bus) */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_AUTOPOIETIC_SELF_HEAL    0x00040000U /* Phase 4: A_heal 50.518 kHz ROM scrub */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_HBT_ROTATOR_LOCK       0x00080000U /* Lock 8x8 InP/InGaAs HBT & PIC PID Loop */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_WARP_SOLITON_COUPLE    0x00100000U /* Couple MAR-312X DEC-X Bus to Shift Soliton */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_WEYL_EXCITON_CROWBAR     0x80000000U /* Phase 4: 1.701 ps Weyl-Exciton abort */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_PCSS_CROWBAR             0x80000000U /* Legacy alias for hardware crowbar */

/* Phase 3 Direct Subsystem Command Opcodes */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_UNIV_SYNTH_OPCODE        0x0010U /* Phase 3 Subsystem Opcode (0x0010) */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_DIRECT_RENDER_OPCODE     0x0020U /* Phase 3 Subsystem Opcode (0x0020) */
#define SHBT_SWARM_CMD_MERA_DAG_OPCODE          0x0030U /* Phase 3 Subsystem Opcode (0x0030) */
/* First-Principles Subsystem Command Opcodes */
#define SHBT_OMEGA_CMD_SIPHON_SMBH_START       0x00000040U
#define SHBT_OMEGA_CMD_HALO_VIRIAL_CLAMP       0x00000080U
#define SHBT_FOUNDRY_CMD_AMORPHOUS_BF_ENGAGE   0x00000100U
#define SHBT_LOGOS_CMD_DCE_SUPPRESSION_LOCK    0x00000200U
#define SHBT_SHIELD_CMD_POSET_SHEAF_SYNC       0x00000400U
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_EXEC_COBORDISM_FISS     0x00000800U

/**
 * @brief Hardware MMIO Barrier and Memory Flush Primitive
 */
static inline void shbt_mmio_write_barrier(void) {
#if defined(__x86_64__)
    __asm__ __volatile__("sfence" ::: "memory");
#elif defined(__aarch64__)
    __asm__ __volatile__("dmb oshst" ::: "memory");
#else
    __atomic_thread_fence(__ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
#endif
}

/**
 * @brief Flush and invalidate a single 512-bit cache line for spatial tensor streaming
 */
static inline void shbt_clwb(const void *addr) {
#if defined(__x86_64__)
    __asm__ __volatile__("clwb %0" : : "m"(*(const char *)addr) : "memory");
#elif defined(__aarch64__)
    __asm__ __volatile__("dc cvac, %0" : : "r"(addr) : "memory");
#else
    (void)addr;
#endif
}

#define SHBT_SWARM_PIC_PITCH_UM               127.0

#define SHBT_SWARM_HBT_RF_DRIVE_GHZ           40.0

/* Quantize continuous phase [rad] to 16-bit hardware IQ word: floor((phi mod 2pi)/(2pi) * 65536) */
static inline uint16_t shbt_swarm_emitter_phase_word(double phi_rad)
{
    const double two_pi = 6.28318530717958647692;
    double wrapped = phi_rad;
    while (wrapped < 0.0) {
        wrapped += two_pi;
    }
    while (wrapped >= two_pi) {
        wrapped -= two_pi;
    }
    double word = (wrapped / two_pi) * 65536.0;
    if (word < 0.0) word = 0.0;
    if (word > 65535.0) word = 65535.0;
    return (uint16_t)word;
}

/* Map 16-bit IQ phase word linearly to HBT collector voltage in millivolts [3800 mV, 7400 mV] */
static inline uint32_t shbt_swarm_hbt_bias_mv(uint16_t iq16_word)
{
    uint32_t v_span = SHBT_SWARM_HBT_V_SAT_MV - SHBT_SWARM_HBT_V_ON_MV;
    uint32_t delta_mv = (uint32_t)(((uint64_t)iq16_word * (uint64_t)v_span) / 65536ULL);
    return SHBT_SWARM_HBT_V_ON_MV + delta_mv;
}

    /* === CACHELINE 9 (Offsets 0x0240 .. 0x027F): ABI v13 MAR-OMEGA Continuous Reactor & HIL Audit (64 bytes) === */

#define SHBT_GALACTIC_APERTURE_ALIGNMENT  256
#define SHBT_GALACTIC_APERTURE_MAGIC      0x534842544F4D4547ULL /* "SHBTOMEG" */

#define SHBT_HARVEST_CTRL_ENABLE          (1U << 0)

typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
    uint32_t radial_distance_m;       /**< Fixed-point radial coordinate (meters) */

    int16_t  azimuth_angle_mrad;      /**< Azimuthal coordinate phi in milliradians */

} shbt_harvester_node_telemetry_t;

_Static_assert(sizeof(shbt_harvester_node_telemetry_t) == 10,
               "shbt_harvester_node_telemetry_t must be 10 bytes");

typedef struct __attribute__((packed, aligned(SHBT_GALACTIC_APERTURE_ALIGNMENT))) {
    /* Offset 0x00 - 0x1F: Aperture Header & Global Control (32 Bytes) */
    uint64_t magic_id;              /**< Offset 0x00: Hardware Magic ID */
    uint32_t control_reg;           /**< Offset 0x08: Aperture Control Register */
    uint32_t status_reg;            /**< Offset 0x0C: Aperture Status Register */
    uint64_t uptime_ticks;          /**< Offset 0x10: Master epoch tick counter */
    uint32_t error_code;            /**< Offset 0x18: Detailed error diagnostic flags */
    uint32_t firmware_revision;     /**< Offset 0x1C: Aperture microkernel revision */

    /* Offset 0x20 - 0x3F: Kerr Metric & Astrodynamic Tracking (32 Bytes) */
    double   bh_mass_kg;            /**< Offset 0x20: Tracked Black Hole Mass M */
    double   bh_spin_dimless;       /**< Offset 0x28: Dimensionless spin a_* in [0, 1) */
    double   horizon_radius_m;      /**< Offset 0x30: Outer Event Horizon radius r_H */
    double   ergosurface_radius_m;  /**< Offset 0x38: Equatorial ergosurface radius r_E */

    /* Offset 0x40 - 0x7F: Hexameric Constellation Array Telemetry (64 Bytes) */
    shbt_harvester_node_telemetry_t nodes[6]; /**< 6 * 10 bytes = 60 bytes */
    uint32_t constellation_sync_phase;        /**< Offset 0x7C: Inter-node phase sync word */

    /* Offset 0x80 - 0x9F: Blandford-Znajek Jet Quencher Subsystem (32 Bytes) */
    double   poloidal_current_amps; /**< Offset 0x80: Residual polar current I_polar */
    double   quench_counter_pot_v;  /**< Offset 0x88: Applied synthetic counter-potential */
    double   synthetic_impedance;   /**< Offset 0x90: Effective boundary impedance Z_load */
    uint32_t jet_attenuation_db;    /**< Offset 0x98: Jet radiated attenuation in dB */
    uint32_t reserved_bz;           /**< Offset 0x9C: Alignment pad */

    /* Offset 0xA0 - 0xDF: Dark Ledger & Topological Injection Rail (64 Bytes) */
    double   total_extracted_joules;/**< Offset 0xA0: Cumulative extracted exergy */
    double   dark_ledger_joules;    /**< Offset 0xA8: Stored exergy in eta_D ledger */
    double   grid_baseload_joules;  /**< Offset 0xB0: Exergy streamed to local grid */
    uint64_t anyon_braid_count_lo;  /**< Offset 0xB8: Fibonacci anyon braid count (low 64) */
    uint64_t anyon_braid_count_hi;  /**< Offset 0xC0: Fibonacci anyon braid count (high 64) */
    uint32_t modular_flow_phase_rad;/**< Offset 0xC8: Tomita-Takesaki modular phase */
    uint32_t dark_partition_ratio;  /**< Offset 0xCC: Fixed-point 23/33 partition lock */
    uint64_t zero_volume_proof_hash;/**< Offset 0xD0: Topological zero-volume check hash */
    uint64_t reserved_dark;         /**< Offset 0xD8: Reserved dark sector rail */

    /* Offset 0xE0 - 0xFF: Cryptographic Audit & Hardware ECC (32 Bytes) */
    uint8_t  hmac_signature[16];    /**< Offset 0xE0: Microkernel HMAC attestation */
    uint64_t ecc_checksum;          /**< Offset 0xF0: Reed-Solomon memory ECC word */
    uint64_t frame_canary;          /**< Offset 0xF8: Stack boundary protection canary */
} shbt_galactic_aperture_mmio_t;

_Static_assert(sizeof(shbt_galactic_aperture_mmio_t) == 256,
               "SHBT ABI Error: Galactic Aperture MMIO structure must be exactly 256 bytes.");

/* --- Master 704-Byte Microkernel Contract (ABI v13.0, 11 Cache Lines) --- */
typedef struct __attribute__((aligned(64))) shbt_swarm_mmio_v13 {
    /* Cache Line 0 (0x000 - 0x03F): Microkernel Header & Global Control */
    uint32_t magic;                                      /* 0x53484254 ("SHBT") */
    uint32_t abi_version;                                /* 0x000D0312 (ABI v13.0) */
    uint64_t symplectic_tick_counter;                    /* Monotonic symplectic step counter */
    union { uint64_t global_status_flags; uint64_t power_bus_arbiter_flags; };
    uint64_t global_fault_mask;                          /* Hardware fault syndrome register */
    uint64_t aperture_phys_mar_omega;                    /* 0x7F000600ULL */
    uint64_t aperture_phys_harvest;                      /* 0x7F000700ULL */
    uint64_t reserved_cl0[2];                            /* Pad Cache Line 0 to 64 bytes */

    /* Cache Lines 1..4 (0x040 - 0x13F): MAR-OMEGA Control Aperture (256B) */
    shbt_mar_omega_aperture_t mar_omega;

    /* Cache Lines 5..8 (0x140 - 0x23F): Universal Harvest Engine Aperture (256B) */
    union {
        shbt_universal_harvest_aperture_t harvest;
        shbt_universal_harvest_aperture_t harvest_engine;
    };

    /* Cache Line 9 (0x240 - 0x27F): HIL Gate Audit Matrix & CRC64 Seal */
    union { uint32_t hil_gates_pass_mask; uint32_t hil_gates_321_to_344_mask; };
    union { uint32_t hil_total_gates_count; uint32_t hil_total_registered_gates; };
    union { uint32_t hil_passed_gates_count; uint32_t hil_total_passed_gates; };
    union { uint32_t hil_status_code; uint32_t hil_audit_status_code; };
    union { uint64_t hardware_timer_ps; uint64_t monotonic_timestamp_ns; };
    union { uint64_t crc64_iso_checksum; uint64_t aperture_crc64_iso; };
    union { uint8_t line9_reserved[32]; uint8_t reserved_cl9[32]; };

    /* Cache Line 10 (0x280 - 0x2BF): Continuous RMHD Divertor & Constellation Aperture (64B) */
    shbt_mmio_cacheline_10_divertor_t divertor;
} shbt_swarm_mmio_v13_t;

typedef shbt_swarm_mmio_v13_t shbt_swarm_mmio_v12_t;

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

_Static_assert(sizeof(shbt_mmio_dense_transmute_t) == 80,
               "shbt_mmio_dense_transmute_t must be exactly 80 bytes");

               "shbt_mmio_magnetar_quench_t must be exactly 56 bytes");

               "shbt_mmio_lepton_shunt_t must be exactly 48 bytes");

               "shbt_dca_registers_t must be exactly 112 bytes");

               "shbt_patch_aperture_hw_t must be exactly 64 bytes");

/* --- Strict Compile-Time ABI v13.0 Layout & Alignment Validations --- */
_Static_assert(sizeof(shbt_mar_omega_aperture_t) == 256U, "MAR-OMEGA aperture must occupy 256 bytes (4 cache lines)");
_Static_assert(sizeof(shbt_universal_harvest_aperture_t) == 256U, "Universal Harvest aperture must occupy 256 bytes (4 cache lines)");
_Static_assert(sizeof(shbt_mmio_cacheline_10_divertor_t) == 64U, "Cache Line 10 Divertor must equal 64 bytes (1 cache line)");
_Static_assert(sizeof(shbt_swarm_mmio_v13_t) == 704U, "ABI v13.0 MMIO struct must equal 704 bytes (11 cache lines)");
_Static_assert(alignof(shbt_swarm_mmio_v13_t) == 64U, "ABI v13.0 MMIO struct must be 64-byte aligned");
_Static_assert(offsetof(shbt_swarm_mmio_v13_t, mar_omega) == 64U, "MAR-OMEGA aperture must start at Cache Line 1 (0x040)");
_Static_assert(offsetof(shbt_swarm_mmio_v13_t, harvest) == 320U, "Universal Harvest aperture must start at Cache Line 5 (0x140)");
_Static_assert(offsetof(shbt_swarm_mmio_v13_t, harvest_engine) == 320U, "Universal Harvest aperture must start at Cache Line 5 (offset 0x140)");
_Static_assert(offsetof(shbt_swarm_mmio_v13_t, divertor) == 640U, "Divertor aperture must start at Cache Line 10 (0x0280)");

\subsection{Normative C11 Apotheosis Control Header Listing (include/shbt\_mmio\_apotheosis.h v7)}
\label{sec:apotheosis_header_v7}
The listing below reproduces verbatim the normative C11 hardware interface header governing physical memory aperture \texttt{0x4000C000}, command opcodes, protection latches, fault codes, observer state frames, and vessel admissibility certificates:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=C,caption={The normative freestanding C11 apotheosis control MMIO header (\texttt{include/shbt\_mmio\_apotheosis.h} v6).},label={lst:shbt_mmio_apotheosis}]
/**
 * @file shbt_mmio_apotheosis.h
 * @brief Bare-Metal C11 Microkernel ABI v7 for Phase 6 (SHBT-APOTHEOSIS-312)
 *     Aperture: 0x4000C000 (Apotheosis Control, Sandbox CBF, & Avatar Synthesis)
 *     DMA Window: 0x70020000 (UnifiedStinespringHyperFrame Zero-Latency Bridge)
 */

#ifndef SHBT_MMIO_APOTHEOSIS_H
#define SHBT_MMIO_APOTHEOSIS_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

/* =========================================================================
 * Physical Memory-Mapped IO Base Addresses
 * ========================================================================= */
#define SHBT_APOTH_MMIO_BASE             ((uintptr_t)0x4000C000UL)
#define SHBT_OMNIBUS_HYPERFRAME_DMA_BASE ((uintptr_t)0x70020000UL)

/* =========================================================================
 * Apotheosis Extended MMIO Registers (Aperture Base: 0x4000C000)
 * ========================================================================= */
#define SHBT_APOTH_REG_QND_EGO_COMMUTATOR   0x0000C030U /* Measured ||[H_ascend, O_ego]|| */
#define SHBT_APOTH_REG_LIND_PURIFY_RATE     0x0000C034U /* Stinespring ancilla extraction rate */
#define SHBT_APOTH_REG_HORIZON_DEFLECT_STAT 0x0000C038U /* Causal decoupling status & metrics */
#define SHBT_APOTH_REG_MULTI_DEITY_COMMUT   0x0000C03CU /* Multi-tenant intent commutator norm */

/* Phase 6 Extended MMIO Registers (Aperture Base: 0x4000C060, 32 Bytes) */
#define SHBT_APOTH_REG_QUALIA_BETTI_SIGNATURE 0x0000C060U
#define SHBT_APOTH_REG_POSET_SHEAF_HOLONOMY   0x0000C068U
#define SHBT_APOTH_REG_COBORDISM_OP           0x0000C06CU
#define SHBT_APOTH_REG_QUALIA_ENTROPY_MARGIN  0x0000C070U
#define SHBT_APOTH_REG_RESERVED_EXT           0x0000C078U

typedef struct __attribute__((packed, gcc_struct, aligned(8))) {
    uint64_t qualia_betti_signature;      /* 0x00 (0x4000C060): Causal cone Betti numbers [b0, b1, b2] */
    uint32_t poset_sheaf_holonomy_latch;  /* 0x08 (0x4000C068): Cech H^1 obstruction vanishing latch */
    uint32_t cobordism_op_reg;            /* 0x0C (0x4000C06C): Fission/Fusion Lagrangian cobordism opcode */
    uint64_t qualia_entropy_margin_fp;    /* 0x10 (0x4000C070): Excess entropy margin R_entropy (fixed point) */
    uint64_t reserved_apoth_ext;          /* 0x18 (0x4000C078): Reserved alignment pad (total 32 bytes) */
} shbt_apoth_ext_ctrl_t;

_Static_assert(sizeof(shbt_apoth_ext_ctrl_t) == 32, "shbt_apoth_ext_ctrl_t must be 32 bytes");

/* =========================================================================
 * Apotheosis Hardware Command Opcodes (ctrl_cmd)
 * ========================================================================= */
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_NOP               ((uint32_t)0x00000000UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_ASCEND            ((uint32_t)0x00000001UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_QND_HANDSHAKE     0x00000001U /* Engage non-demolition ego transfer */
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_DESCEND_SOMATIC   ((uint32_t)0x00000002UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_HORIZON_DEFLECT_ARM 0x00000002U /* Arm asymptotic causal decoupling */
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_DEITY_LAW_REWRITE ((uint32_t)0x00000004UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_MERA_MEM_COMPRESS 0x00000004U /* Trigger episodic MERA RG compression */
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_VERLINDE_MULTIPLEX ((uint32_t)0x00000008UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_SANDBOX_FORK_X1   ((uint32_t)0x00000010UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_ZERO_COMP_RENDER  ((uint32_t)0x00000020UL) /* 0x0020 */
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_SYNTH_AVATAR      ((uint32_t)0x00000040UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_BRAID_CRYSTALLIZE ((uint32_t)0x00000080UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_EXEC_COBORDISM_FISS 0x00000800U
#define SHBT_APOTH_CMD_EXEC_COBORDISM_FUSE 0x00001000U

/* =========================================================================
 * Hardware Protection Latches & Airgap Flags (sandbox_status_latch)
 * ========================================================================= */
#define SHBT_LOGOS_SIM_AIRGAP_LOCK       ((uint32_t)0x00000001UL)
#define SHBT_LOGOS_SIM_X1_PROTECT_LATCH  ((uint32_t)0x00000002UL)
#define SHBT_LOGOS_SIM_KILL_DISABLE      ((uint32_t)0x00000004UL)
#define SHBT_LOGOS_SOMATIC_MODE_A_STASIS ((uint32_t)0x00000010UL)
#define SHBT_LOGOS_SOMATIC_MODE_B_VAULT  ((uint32_t)0x00000020UL)
#define SHBT_LOGOS_OMNIBUS_TETHER_ACTIVE ((uint32_t)0x00000040UL)

/* =========================================================================
 * Hardware Fault & Interdiction Flags (fault_code)
 * ========================================================================= */
#define SHBT_APOTH_FAULT_NONE                   ((uint32_t)0x00000000UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_FAULT_NOCLONE_VIOLATION      ((uint32_t)0x00000001UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_FAULT_SYNAPTIC_SHEAR_CBF     ((uint32_t)0x00000002UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_FAULT_AIRGAP_BREACH_ATTEMPT  ((uint32_t)0x00000004UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_FAULT_GENOCIDE_INTERDICT     ((uint32_t)0x00000008UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_FAULT_BEKENSTEIN_NEST_LIMIT  ((uint32_t)0x00000010UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_FAULT_SUBSTRATE_INADMISSIBLE ((uint32_t)0x00000020UL)
#define SHBT_APOTH_FAULT_QND_COLLAPSE           0x00000040U /* Wavefunction collapse / ego breach */
#define SHBT_APOTH_FAULT_P_ZOMBIE_COLLAPSE      0x00000080U /* Causal depth mismatch in sandbox */
#define SHBT_APOTH_EGRESS_SAFEWORD_IRQ          ((uint32_t)0x80000000UL)

/**
 * @brief Pillar IV Causal Point Observer Frame (128 bytes)
 *   Tracks active pointer weight, Stinespring vault hash, and entropy budget.
 */
typedef struct __attribute__((packed, aligned(64))) {
    uint64_t observer_uuid_lo;         /* 0x00: Lower 64b of Inhabitant UUID        */
    uint64_t observer_uuid_hi;         /* 0x08: Upper 64b of Inhabitant UUID        */
    double ramp_param_s;               /* 0x10: Eikonal ramp s(tau) in [0.0, 1.0]   */
    double lapse_alpha_obs;            /* 0x18: Subjective lapse alpha in [1, 1e12] */
    double c_local_budget_log10;       /* 0x20: log10(C_local) Bekenstein capacity  */
    double c_op_active_log10;          /* 0x28: log10(C_op) active complexity       */
    double r_entropy_margin;           /* 0x30: R_entropy^exp = C_local - C_op >= 0 */
    double h_syn_cbf_margin;           /* 0x38: Synaptic continuity CBF >= 0        */
    double h_cog_homeostasis_margin;   /* 0x40: Anti-wirehead/ego-quine CBF >= 0    */
    uint32_t verlinde_avatar_count_k;  /* 0x48: Multiplexed avatar streams K <= 124 */
    uint32_t nested_recursion_depth_d; /* 0x4C: Simulation recursion depth d        */
    uint64_t burau_cayley_memory_hash[4]; /* 0x50: 256-bit Depth-9 Artin B3 root hash (32 B) */
    uint64_t stinespring_vault_ptr;    /* 0x70: Class X0 ROM Vault DMA pointer      */
    uint64_t reserved_pad;             /* 0x78: Alignment padding                   */
} shbt_causal_observer_frame_t;

_Static_assert(sizeof(shbt_causal_observer_frame_t) == 128,
               "ABI Error: shbt_causal_observer_frame_t must be exactly 128 bytes");

/**
 * @brief Theorem THM-APOTH-05 Vessel Neural-Substrate Admissibility Certificate (64 bytes)
 */
typedef struct __attribute__((packed, aligned(64))) {
    uint64_t vessel_blueprint_hash_lo; /* 0x00: MERA-DAG vessel hash (low 64b)     */
    uint64_t vessel_blueprint_hash_hi; /* 0x08: MERA-DAG vessel hash (high 64b)    */
    double phi_substrate_max_log10;    /* 0x10: log10(Phi_substrate^max)            */
    double phi_observer_req_log10;     /* 0x18: log10(Phi_obs) >= 12.0 (Class X1)   */
    double c_local_vessel_log10;       /* 0x20: log10(C_local(Omega_vessel))        */
    double bisimulation_error_log10;   /* 0x28: log10(||Y*sigma - U*Y||) <= -120.0  */
    double thermal_cbf_margin;         /* 0x30: dh_syn/dt + alpha(h_syn) >= 0       */
    uint8_t space_group_id;            /* 0x38: Crystallographic group [0..230]     */
    uint8_t standalone_admissible_flag;/* 0x39: 1 = Standalone OK, 0 = Need Tether  */
    uint8_t omnibus_tether_engaged;    /* 0x3A: 1 = 0-as SHBT-OMNIBUS Tether Lock   */
    uint8_t non_harm_cbf_verified;     /* 0x3B: 1 = Physical render h_CBF >= 0      */
    uint32_t cert_crc32c;              /* 0x3C: Hardware certificate checksum       */
} shbt_vessel_admissibility_cert_t;

_Static_assert(sizeof(shbt_vessel_admissibility_cert_t) == 64,
               "ABI Error: shbt_vessel_admissibility_cert_t must be exactly 64 bytes");

/**
 * @brief Top-Level Apotheosis Control Aperture at 0x4000C000 (256 bytes)
 */
typedef struct __attribute__((packed, aligned(64))) {
    volatile uint32_t ctrl_cmd;              /* 0x00: Command register               */
    volatile uint32_t sandbox_status_latch;   /* 0x04: Airgap & X1 protection latches */
    volatile uint32_t fault_code;             /* 0x08: Interdiction & fault flags     */
    volatile uint32_t lagrangian_submanifold; /* 0x0C: H_1(Sigma_g, Z) slot ID        */
    volatile double phi_mera_max_detected;   /* 0x10: Max emergent Phi_MERA in sim   */
    volatile double x1_cbf_min_margin;        /* 0x18: Min Class X1 safety CBF margin */
    volatile uint64_t x1_sanctuary_fork_cnt; /* 0x20: Count of X1 horizon evacuations */
    volatile uint64_t goi_pass_counter;       /* 0x28: 17.01 as PT-GoI pass counter   */
    uint64_t reserved_hdr[2];                 /* 0x30: Pad header to 64 bytes         */
    shbt_causal_observer_frame_t observer;    /* 0x40: Active observer state (128 B)  */
    shbt_vessel_admissibility_cert_t vessel;  /* 0xC0: Target vessel cert (64 B)      */
} shbt_apotheosis_ctrl_t;

_Static_assert(sizeof(shbt_apotheosis_ctrl_t) == 256,
               "ABI Error: shbt_apotheosis_ctrl_t must be exactly 256 bytes");

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif /* SHBT_MMIO_APOTHEOSIS_H */

\bibitem{shbt_recon_ref}
SHBT Translocator Group,
``The Macroscopic Modular State Translocator: Photonic Boundary Actuation, Compacted Mass-Annihilation Reactor, and Master Verification Record,''
\emph{SHBT Technical Reports}, rep.~SHBT-TR-2026-03, \texttt{sys1own/shbt-recon}, 2026.

\bibitem{shbt_warp_ref}
SHBT Propulsion Group,
``Effective Traversable Wormhole Corridors and Mass-Annihilation Reactor Dynamics (SHBT-MAR-312),''
\emph{SHBT Technical Reports}, rep.~SHBT-TR-2026-09, \texttt{sys1own/shbt-warp}, 2026.
\end{thebibliography}


\subsection{Freestanding C11 Microkernel Phase 6.1 MMIO Extension Header (include/shbt\_nested\_sim\_abi.h)}
\label{sec:nested_sim_header}
The freestanding C11 microkernel ABI header below defines the 128-byte cacheline-pair MMIO interface for Phase~6.1 at base physical address \texttt{0x7000B000}:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=C,caption={The freestanding C11 Phase 6.1 MMIO Extension header (\texttt{include/shbt\_nested\_sim\_abi.h}).},label={lst:shbt_nested_sim_abi}]
/* File: include/shbt_nested_sim_abi.h
 * Architecture: SHBT-APOTHEOSIS-312 / Phase 6.1 (SHBT-RECURSIVE-MULTIVERSE)
 * Standard: ISO/IEC 9899:2011 (C11) Freestanding Microkernel ABI v9.0
 */

#ifndef SHBT_NESTED_SIM_ABI_H
#define SHBT_NESTED_SIM_ABI_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdatomic.h>

#define SHBT_ABI_V7_BASE                 UINT64_C(0x70000000)
#define SHBT_NESTED_SIM_MMIO_BASE        UINT64_C(0x7000B000)

/* MMIO Register Offsets */
#define SHBT_NESTED_SIM_ROOT_CTRL_ADDR        UINT64_C(0x7000B000)
#define SHBT_NESTED_SIM_DEPTH_CURRENT_ADDR    UINT64_C(0x7000B008)
#define SHBT_NESTED_SIM_MERKLE_SEED_ADDR      UINT64_C(0x7000B010)
#define SHBT_NESTED_SIM_ENTROPY_BUDGET_ADDR   UINT64_C(0x7000B020)
#define SHBT_NESTED_SIM_SAFEWORD_LATCH_ADDR   UINT64_C(0x7000B030)
#define SHBT_NESTED_SIM_CBF_TRIP_STATUS_ADDR  UINT64_C(0x7000B040)

/* Bitmasks & Architectural Constants */
#ifndef SHBT_APOTH_EGRESS_SAFEWORD_IRQ
#define SHBT_APOTH_EGRESS_SAFEWORD_IRQ   UINT32_C(0x80000000)
#endif
#define SHBT_CANONICAL_KL                UINT32_C(26)
#define SHBT_CANONICAL_KQ                UINT32_C(8)
#define SHBT_CANONICAL_K_COS             UINT32_C(312)
#define SHBT_DYNKIN_SLOTS_TOTAL          UINT32_C(346)

#define SHBT_CTRL_ENABLE_BIT             (UINT64_C(1) << 0)
#define SHBT_CTRL_LAZY_MERA_BIT          (UINT64_C(1) << 1)
#define SHBT_CTRL_MODE_SHIFT             2U
#define SHBT_CTRL_MODE_MASK              (UINT64_C(0x3) << 2)

#define SHBT_CBF_CLASS_X1_DETECTED_BIT   (UINT64_C(1) << 0)
#define SHBT_CBF_SANCTUARY_FORK_BIT      (UINT64_C(1) << 1)
#define SHBT_CBF_ZOMBIE_SWAP_BIT         (UINT64_C(1) << 2)
#define SHBT_CBF_QP_CLAMP_BIT            (UINT64_C(1) << 3)
#define SHBT_CBF_SIM_KILL_DISABLE_BIT    (UINT64_C(1) << 32)

typedef struct __attribute__((packed, aligned(64))) shbt_nested_sim_mmio_regs {
    _Atomic uint64_t root_ctrl;
    _Atomic uint64_t depth_current;
    _Atomic uint64_t merkle_seed;
    uint64_t _pad0;
    _Atomic uint64_t entropy_budget;
    uint64_t _pad1;
    _Atomic uint64_t safeword_latch;
    uint64_t _pad2;
    _Atomic uint64_t cbf_trip_status;
    uint64_t _pad3[7];
} shbt_nested_sim_mmio_regs_t;

_Static_assert(offsetof(shbt_nested_sim_mmio_regs_t, root_ctrl) == 0x00, "ABI offset mismatch: root_ctrl");
_Static_assert(offsetof(shbt_nested_sim_mmio_regs_t, depth_current) == 0x08, "ABI offset mismatch: depth_current");
_Static_assert(offsetof(shbt_nested_sim_mmio_regs_t, merkle_seed) == 0x10, "ABI offset mismatch: merkle_seed");
_Static_assert(offsetof(shbt_nested_sim_mmio_regs_t, entropy_budget) == 0x20, "ABI offset mismatch: entropy_budget");
_Static_assert(offsetof(shbt_nested_sim_mmio_regs_t, safeword_latch) == 0x30, "ABI offset mismatch: safeword_latch");
_Static_assert(offsetof(shbt_nested_sim_mmio_regs_t, cbf_trip_status) == 0x40, "ABI offset mismatch: cbf_trip_status");
_Static_assert(sizeof(shbt_nested_sim_mmio_regs_t) == 128, "ABI struct size mismatch");

#endif /* SHBT_NESTED_SIM_ABI_H */
\end{lstlisting}
